Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.

 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:

SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:

SWRL Rule:

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 <!-- http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor -->

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:

SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.

 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
Patient(?p) ^ ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?plan) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ observedForPatient(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> sendNotificationToDoctor(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ WalkingActivity(?a) ^ performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ SmartWatch(?sw) ^ saref:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ Doctor(?d) -> sendNotificationTo(?p, ?d)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ Tremor(?t) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ observes(?obs, ?t) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) -> notificationSentToRelatives(?p, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ Observation(?obs) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?notif) -> sendNotification(?notif, true) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ PD_Patient(?patient) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?patient) ^ NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^ NotifyRelatives(?relativeNotification) -> sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^ sendNotification(?relativeNotification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ isMonitored(?p, true) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^ Dopamine(?d) ^ contains(?mp, ?d) -> ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ sosa:FeatureOfInterest(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ NotifyDoctor(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^ hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^ hasDosePlan(?p, ?dp) ^ hasFrequency(?dp, ?freq) ^ swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> sendNotificationToDoctorForAction(?p, true)
